# LAMBDA_SOURCE_MAP_CONSUMER_CENSUS -- CT-bb-1 (stage 1, read-only)

Generated by `source_map_census.py` (this folder). Reads the committed tree at HEAD through git; no
working-copy reads, no network, no write outside this folder. Scope: the whole repository.
Map: `infra/aws/lambda/ci/lambda-source-map.json`. Deploy script: `infra/aws/lambda/ci/record-terraform-deploy.sh`.

Method: the line patterns class every file that names the map (window of +/-4 lines around each hit; a comment line is classed by its own text; a document extension is DOC). The worker then read every READ_WHOLE, WRITE and undecided file: every line naming the map plus every line that opens, loads or writes a file, at HEAD (`git show HEAD:<path>`; the worktree is sparse, so the whole repository is read from the commit, never from the working copy). A reading overrides the pattern class; a file neither the patterns nor the reading could class is OTHER.

## 3. The map's own shape (HEAD)

- bytes: 439287; top-level keys: 5 (_description, _updated, _note, functions, _last_deploy_recorded)
- metadata keys (not functions): _description, _updated, _note, _last_deploy_recorded
- function entries (keys of `functions`): **240**
- distinct key sets across entries: 99; union of entry keys: 53
- entry size (json indent 2, bytes): min 91, median 657.0, max 36445
- Terraform, the two counts: (a) the .tf files that NAME the map (40 files) declare **7** `aws_lambda_function` resources; (b) the whole repository's committed .tf files declare **73** `aws_lambda_function` resources in 47 files.
- difference, map keys (240) minus (b) repository Terraform resources (73): 167; minus (a): 233
- the map is not one-to-one with the Terraform resources: a function may have a map row and no resource (a deployed-only or image-built row), or a resource and no row; this is measured, not reconciled, here

Union of entry keys: _added_note, _artifact_note, _b1_note, _build_note, _deploy_note, _deploy_note_ap9, _deploy_record_correction, _files_note, _g3_note, _not_reproducible, _note, _packaging, _parity_note, _pre_stage, _pre_stage_blocker, _staged_from_note, _tf_note, artifact_s3_bucket, artifact_s3_key, artifact_sha256, artifact_sha256_provenance, build_script, build_type, deploy_ledger, deploy_note, deployed_code_sha256, files, handler, iam_role, image_uri, inline_archive_file, last_deployed_at, last_deployed_commit, layers, memory_size, note, notes, output_dir, outside_package, package_type, pipeline, recorded_by, recorded_reason, runtime, s3_bucket, s3_key, sha256, source, source_dir, staged_from, tf_file, tf_resource, timeout

Example entry (`mdpf-gold-lambda`, first key in file order):

```json
{
  "mdpf-gold-lambda": {
    "source_dir": "infra/aws/lambda/mdpf-pipeline/mdpf-gold-lambda",
    "files": [
      "build_package.py"
    ],
    "tf_file": "infra/aws/terraform/mdpf-pipeline/gold-lambda.tf",

    "iam_role": "mdpf-gold-lambda-role",
    "_build_note": "MDPF_CANPLAT.P1-2: package is ASSEMBLED FROM A PINNED COMMIT by build_package.py (git show <pin>:<path> per module; 22 entries at the 70a0828226 pin: 18 modules incl. validate_interpolated_backfill + mdpf_category_registry.py + lib/trading_calendar.py AS trading_calendar.py, 3 configs, + r7_comparator.py/sectionB_field_paths.json (count corrected 2026-08-28: the note said 19 while PACKAGE declared 22), FLAT at zip root; BUILD_MANIFEST.json inside carries per-module sha256 + config hashes). source_dir holds the BUILDER, not the runtime source -- the runtime source is the pinned repo commit recorded below and inside BUILD_MANIFEST.json. Deps: pandas-pyarrow-requests-layer (existing) + mdpf-gold-calendar-deps (pmc<5 pin, load-bearing). DEPLOYED UNWIRED (zero triggers) until the floor re-point act.",
    "artifact_s3_key": "portal/gold/mdpf/_code/mdpf-gold-lambda.zip",

    "artifact_sha256": "bnpYayh00uIIIlZEMjc3pKmKz6bFcuHGY6F2DI4HZTY=",
    "last_deployed_commit": "70a08282269217b3ccae4bb2776372690201743f",

  }
}
```
